# Install the hook pack

`examples/` is the source of truth for every host adapter. This document
packages those files plus `hooks/run.ts` (and the fail-closed HTTP wrappers)
as the install unit. Per-host deny JSON and honest limits:
[docs/hosts.md](hosts.md). The Next workbench is optional once hooks can
reach the scorer.

RH Guard is **not** [Augustus](https://github.com/24601/Augustus). Augustus
places typed System One judgments. This pack is the live hazard gate on agent
tools. It is also **not** [jevgate](https://github.com/thevibeworks/jevgate):
an allowlist proves what may run, Jev judges only the rest, and the tool
cannot block. Same layering as structural deny + sidecar, different job.

## 1. Sidecar (required for live scoring)

From a clone of [24601/rh-guard](https://github.com/24601/rh-guard):

```bash
git clone https://github.com/24601/rh-guard.git
cd rh-guard
npm install
cp .env.example .env.local   # set TYPESAFE_API_KEY for Jev
npm run dev                  # 127.0.0.1:43147
```

Without a key the process still listens. Structural denies still fire.
Lexical / GLiClass-shaped scoring is degraded, not risk zero.

## 2. Claude Code

### Project settings (copy from examples/)

- HTTP-only (prompt + Stop + PreToolUse): merge
  [`examples/claude-settings.json`](../examples/claude-settings.json) into
  `.claude/settings.json`.
- Fail-closed PreToolUse: merge
  [`examples/claude-command-settings.json`](../examples/claude-command-settings.json)
  instead. The command `npx tsx hooks/run.ts claude` must run with this repo
  as the working tree (or otherwise resolve `hooks/run.ts` and `src/`).

Claude HTTP hooks only honor a 2xx JSON body. Timeouts and non-2xx do not
block.

### Plugin marketplace (skill + hook pack)

```bash
claude plugin marketplace add 24601/rh-guard
claude plugin install rh-guard@rh-guard
```

Layout:

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` | Marketplace catalog |
| `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` | Plugin manifest (points at the skill + `hooks/hooks.json`) |
| `hooks/hooks.json` | Claude plugin hook config (same events/matcher/URL as `examples/`) |
| `hooks/claude-hook.sh` | Fail-closed PreToolUse: POST stdin to the sidecar, deny + exit 2 on failure |
| `hooks/run.ts` | In-repo command scorer (`claude` / `cursor` / `grok` / `generic`; aliases `codex`, `dsh`, `exo`) |
| `hooks/grok-hook.sh` | Fail-closed Grok PreToolUse (host is fail-open on crash/timeout) |
| `.agents/skills/rh-guard/SKILL.md` | Protocol skill (not the server) |

`hooks/hooks.json` uses HTTP for `UserPromptSubmit` and `Stop` (fail-open if
the sidecar is down) and `hooks/claude-hook.sh` for `PreToolUse` (fail-closed).
Override the sidecar URL with `RH_GUARD_URL` if you do not bind
`127.0.0.1:43147` (`HACK_RADAR_URL` is still accepted).

Keep the sidecar running. The plugin does not start Next.js.

## 3. Cursor (hooks.json is the plugin)

There is no Cursor marketplace equivalent. Copy
[`examples/cursor-hooks.json`](../examples/cursor-hooks.json) to
`.cursor/hooks.json`.

```bash
cp examples/cursor-hooks.json /path/to/your-project/.cursor/hooks.json
```

Command hooks read JSON on stdin via `npx tsx hooks/run.ts cursor`. That
command must resolve this repo (cloud agents run project hooks from the repo
root; use a checkout or submodule of rh-guard, or change the command to an
absolute path).

Set `failClosed: true` on shell and tool gates, as in the example.
`beforeSubmitPrompt` cannot inject context: a gameable prompt is allowed with
a user notice. Tool-denial steering uses a generic `agent_message` that does
not leak scores.

## 4. Other hosts (same scorer)

Copy the file in `examples/` for that host. Details: [docs/hosts.md](hosts.md).

| Host | Copy |
|---|---|
| Codex | [`examples/codex-hooks.json`](../examples/codex-hooks.json) → `~/.codex/hooks.json` or `.codex/hooks.json`. Command PreToolUse only. |
| Grok Build | [`examples/grok-hooks.json`](../examples/grok-hooks.json) → `~/.grok/hooks/` or `.grok/hooks/`. Prefer `hooks/grok-hook.sh` for fail-closed. |
| Pi | [`examples/pi-extension.ts`](../examples/pi-extension.ts) → `~/.pi/agent/extensions/` or `.pi/extensions/`. Optional command settings: [`examples/pi-hooks-settings.json`](../examples/pi-hooks-settings.json) with `@hsingjui/pi-hooks` (no HTTP). |
| Amp | [`examples/amp-plugin.ts`](../examples/amp-plugin.ts) → `.amp/plugins/` or `~/.config/amp/plugins/`. |
| Prime Agent | [`examples/prime-extension.ts`](../examples/prime-extension.ts) → `~/.prime/agent/extensions/` or `.prime/agent/extensions/`. |
| dsh | DeepSeek Harness **generic stdin**: `hooks/run.ts dsh` or `generic` with [`examples/generic-event.json`](../examples/generic-event.json). HTTP skipped (404). Claude/Codex **command-hook** bridges at `tools/pre-execute` also work. |
| Exo | **support via ToolRuntime wrap**, not drop-in hooks; no native `hooks.json`. [`examples/exo-tool-runtime.ts`](../examples/exo-tool-runtime.ts) / `.rs`. Optional `.exo/agent-tools/` gate: [`examples/exo-agent-tools-gate.ts`](../examples/exo-agent-tools-gate.ts). |

Pi / Amp / Prime copies are self-contained (duplicated `AGENT_DENY` + `fetch`) so they run outside this repo. They POST to `/api/hooks/<flavor>`; keep the sidecar running.

## 5. Companion skill (any skills-compatible agent)

```bash
npx skills add 24601/rh-guard --skill rh-guard
```

Use the skill to design evals, interpret denials, and choose gates. Do not
use it as a runbook for `next dev`.

## 6. What not to do

- Do not train RL against this monitor or against chain-of-thought.
- Do not put hidden tests in a workspace file the agent can edit.
- Do not treat lexical scores as a Jev ROC.
- Do not treat [Harbor](https://github.com/harbor-framework/harbor), [jevals](https://github.com/dayhaysoos/jevals), or [openevals](https://github.com/memovai/openevals) as required to install the hooks (practices, not install dependencies). See [eval-integrity.md](eval-integrity.md).
- Do not treat [pi-jev-approver](https://github.com/phin-tech/pi-jev-approver), [agent-workflow-typesafe-ai](https://github.com/ngallodev-software/agent-workflow-typesafe-ai), Do not merge [decision-kernel](https://github.com/Agentic-Guides/decision-kernel), [kev-model-router](https://github.com/NeOMakinG/kev-model-router), [eugeniughelbur/jev-gate](https://github.com/eugeniughelbur/jev-gate), or [gentle-browser-jev](https://github.com/jorgehara/gentle-browser-jev) into `examples/`.
- [vishalbitit/jev-prior-auth-triage](https://github.com/vishalbitit/jev-prior-auth-triage) advertised prior-auth triage ships `README.md`, `pipeline/triage.py`, `policy/policies.py`, `data/*`, and `analysis/*`. No license. Quoted **Jev never denies anything.** Routes are `auto_approve`, `pend_clinical_review`, and `peer_to_peer_required`. `auto_approve` is a route, not a permission. `auto_approve` is not a hard grant. Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Distinct from [bhaskarpraveen/jev-healthcare-support-router](https://github.com/bhaskarpraveen/jev-healthcare-support-router). Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [benjamincanac/tia](https://github.com/benjamincanac/tia) GitHub issue triage. Quoted **It never closes, transfers or converts an issue.** **Providers without a distribution count as certain.** `apply_triage` is the only writer. `dryRun` defaults true. A label is not a close. Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [satiricalguru/Fast-Jev-Agents](https://github.com/satiricalguru/Fast-Jev-Agents) verbatim compaction. Quoted **Default 'throw' for strict mode.** The Claude hook fail-opens to built-in compaction. 50/50 passing is not a rh-guard ROC. Prune is not deny. Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [Alberto-Codes/judgevet](https://github.com/Alberto-Codes/judgevet) typed client, CLI, and MCP. Quoted **The response shape is verified.** Tools `ask_noul`, `ask_choice`, `ask_score`. If the agent never calls the tools, no gate runs. The 90% coverage floor is not a rh-guard ROC. Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [Ivanovskyi/typesafe-ai-gateway](https://github.com/Ivanovskyi/typesafe-ai-gateway) Spring confidence router. Threshold 0.75. `executeMockService` is not a live dispatch. Routing is not permission. Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [SAITS-Swiss-AI-Tech-Services/jev-mcp](https://github.com/SAITS-Swiss-AI-Tech-Services/jev-mcp) browser-agent MCP. Quoted **There is no blocklist.** The domain lock is code. Distinct from [emlama/jev-mcp](https://github.com/emlama/jev-mcp). Do not merge into `examples/`.
- [aleksvega/jev-skill-router](https://github.com/aleksvega/jev-skill-router) skill router and auditor. Quoted **A broken router never blocks your agent.** The advertised exit 2 is not the served exit. `--init` prints the paste rule and does not write the file. Choice over at most 60 skills. [shubhamjoshipromail-svg/opspilot-ai](https://github.com/shubhamjoshipromail-svg/opspilot-ai) (no license file, Python, 4 stars, HEAD `9abcbd3`) routes support tickets with fine-tuned ModernBERT. **Not TypeSafe Jev.** Quoted README: **Decision support for human operators. Not autonomous action.** `routing.py` sends escalation risk >= **0.80** to `human_review` and policy-sensitive text to `supervisor_review` before it trusts category confidence; below **0.65** is also `human_review`. The remaining path is `auto_triage_suggestion`. *Theirs* validation: ModernBERT accuracy **0.7313**, weighted-F1 **0.7127**, ECE **0.0841**; at **0.80** it auto-routes **61.8%** of rows at **86.5%** accuracy on those rows. Zero-shot Jev on the same rows: accuracy **0.5458**, below the majority baseline **0.5946**, ECE **0.2896**; where it reported **0.98**, accuracy was **0.65**. Quoted: read the taxonomy column before the accuracy column (7-class versus 10-class). Escalation labels are policy heuristics, not observed outcomes. `reference_answer` is excluded from inputs. Those figures are not a rh-guard ROC and not a SWE-hack claim. Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.
[Teylersf/WindowsJev](https://github.com/Teylersf/WindowsJev) (MIT, C#, `net8.0-windows`, HEAD `549497e`) is a local stdio MCP server for Codex and Claude Code. Quoted SECURITY.md: **Jev selects only action identifiers constructed by deterministic code.** `ForbiddenWords` (password, pay, delete, send, publish) return `blocked` before a model call. MCP sets `AllowDestructive` **false**. Defaults: `MinimumConfidence` **0.45**, `DestructiveMinimumConfidence` **0.90**, `MaxSteps` **12** (clamped to **20**). A done needs `GoalCompleteProbability` >= **0.70**. No key returns `configuration_required` and does not run the loop. `DecideAsync` is not caught, so a thrown TypeSafe call does not execute. Claim checks set `RequiresReview` when confidence < **0.80**. Those floors are uncalibrated. URL and search fast paths spend no Jev tokens. If the host never calls the tool, no gate runs. The binary is unsigned. Distinct from [brnyxx/jev-ra](https://github.com/brnyxx/jev-ra) and [amidabuddha/jev-decision-mcp](https://github.com/amidabuddha/jev-decision-mcp). Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.
[Tom-R-Main/Footwork](https://github.com/Tom-R-Main/Footwork) (MIT, Python and Rust, package `jevdual`, HEAD `37b923b`) is a verified browser agent. Description rewrite (*theirs*): a cheap Jev guard (evidence-checked completions, a destructive gate) in front of any LLM browser driver, with Jev taking the mechanical steps in dual mode. Built on browser-use **0.13.10**. `arbiter.toml`: `act_operation_confidence` **0.55**, `act_target_confidence` **0.45**, `goal_done_escalate` **0.85** (**S1 never declares victory alone**), and `destructive_confirm` **0.5** with a keyword list are **never auto-executed**. Quoted README: every threshold is a starting point; **none has been tuned on heldout data.** The file comment still says E1 tunes on the dev split and then freezes. A done needs the `complete` noul and every `unmet` noul inside the accept band, quoting evidence atoms. The LLM judge is recorded and is never the grade. *Theirs* fixture heldout: stock **9/10** with one false completion (deleted an account); dual **10/10** with **0** false completions. On 55 live tasks, guarded and dual had **0** false completions; stock's own judge accepted one. Those figures are not a rh-guard ROC. A mechanical step at **0.55** is still a soft act on the offered menu. Distinct from [brnyxx/jev-ra](https://github.com/brnyxx/jev-ra), [browser-use/jev-ultrafast](https://github.com/browser-use/jev-ultrafast), and [matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate). Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.
[1aifanatic/uipath-maestroflow-jev](https://github.com/1aifanatic/uipath-maestroflow-jev) (no license file, HEAD `8d2e1c`) is a Maestro Flow card-dispute triage bench. Quoted: **Hard rules precede anything either model said.** R-1 (`age_days > 60`) is date arithmetic and never a model call. R-6 default is `ANALYST_REVIEW`. Quoted: **Not a compliance artefact** and not legal advice. Synthetic ten emails. Quoted: **R-2 never fires on this dataset.** `jev-1.13.0` reason-code confidence is **1.00** on eight of ten; the intended trigger returns **0.75**, so **0.60 is below anything this model produces here.** R-4 still grants `PROVISIONAL_CREDIT_FRAUD` when `unauthorized_claim.noul > 0.80` and the reason code matches (soft-as-grant on that branch). *Theirs* on 2026-09-22: Jev leg **34.4 s** versus agent **105.8 s**, ratio **2.72×**, ten disputes **$0.000419**. Quoted: sample size is ten; **illustrative by design, not a rigorous benchmark.** Those figures are not a rh-guard ROC. Thresholds are starting points. Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.
[RavioliCodes/jev-support-agents](https://github.com/RavioliCodes/jev-support-agents) (MIT, Python, HEAD `160b77c`) is a support API where the LLMs write and Jev routes. Description rewrite (*theirs*): typed decisions for routing, response evaluation, and retry or escalation, plus a 60-ticket benchmark. No key, a thrown call, or confidence below `JEV_MIN_CONFIDENCE` (default **0.6**) returns HUMAN. `SAFE_TO_SEND_THRESHOLD` is **0.8**. `apply_safety_policy` turns ACCEPT into ESCALATE when `safe_to_send` is false or `next_action` confidence is low; a Jev failure is ESCALATE. `MAX_RETRIES = 2` is a code loop. *Theirs* on 60 hand-labelled tickets, `jev-latest` serving `jev-1.13.0`: routing **93.3%** versus free-text **70.0%** and structured **73.3%**; acceptance **29.2%** versus about **100%**; escalation **75.0%**, including **71.2%** of answerable tickets; **29** ACCEPT decisions overridden. Quoted: acceptance is each decider's own evaluator, **not the true quality of the response.** Those figures are not a rh-guard ROC. A route is not a grant. Distinct from [shubhamjoshipromail-svg/opspilot-ai](https://github.com/shubhamjoshipromail-svg/opspilot-ai) and [deepansh-saxena/jev-guardrails](https://github.com/deepansh-saxena/jev-guardrails). Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.
[bansal1600/jev-as-a-judge-benchmarking](https://github.com/bansal1600/jev-as-a-judge-benchmarking) (Apache-2.0, Python, HEAD `d4862ac`) grades frozen support replies with TypeSafe Jev, gpt-4o, gpt-5.6-terra, and a local gemma judge. Quoted: **No human oracle.** This measures agreement and reliability, **never accuracy.** The rubric is not held constant. Routing columns sit at 100% by construction and cannot rank judges. *Theirs* on 14 replies times 2 reps: `jev-latest` **67.2%**, gpt-4o **66.1%**, gpt-5.6-terra **65.2%**, gemma4 **79.5%**. Quoted: treat sub-5pp gaps as noise. `jev-latest` is a moving alias. Those figures are not a rh-guard ROC. Using the judge as a reward is the failure mode this gate is meant to catch. Distinct from [danielgshea/jev-as-a-judge](https://github.com/danielgshea/jev-as-a-judge). Soft judgment is never the sole veto. Do not merge into `examples/`. Cousin, not this sidecar.

[hermes-nerve](https://github.com/keeltrace/hermes-nerve) (MIT, Python, HEAD `de219b1`, 12 stars; current listing of [keeltrace/hermes-jev](https://github.com/keeltrace/hermes-jev), which redirects here) is Nerve — a supervisory System-1 layer for Hermes agents (typed decisions, ranking, verification, token-aware oversight, opt-in tool gate) over TypeSafe Jev or open Laya/OpenJev Reflex backends. Quoted README: Nerve/Reflex is a **watchdog and forecaster**; the main Hermes orchestrator owns stop/continue. `gate_mode` default **off**; `gate_scope` default **selective**; `min_confidence` **0.80**. Quoted SECURITY.md: **Do not treat a Jev probability as proof of correctness.** Context engine is opt-in and fails open. Catalog template **v0.2.1.2**; development line **v0.2.2** (dev17). Live suite figures remain **not a rh-guard ROC**. Cousin of hermes-plugin-jev / typesafe-jev-gate / hermes-jev-skills, not this sidecar.

- Do not send `continue: false` on Codex PreToolUse (Codex fails the hook and continues the tool).
- Do not deny Amp with `action: "error"` or by throwing (Amp ignores thrown plugin errors).
- Do not reuse Claude stdout for Grok (`{decision:deny,reason}` only).
- Do not treat Exo as drop-in hooks; wrap `ToolRuntime.execute` / `executeTool`.
- Do not invent `exo-hooks.json`; Exo has no native hook file.
